20.     public sealed partial class Scenario4 : Page
21.     {
22.         private TransformGroup transforms;
23.         private MatrixTransform previousTransform;
24.         private CompositeTransform deltaTransform;
25.         private bool forceManipulationsToEnd;
26. 
27.         public Scenario4()
28.         {
29.             this.InitializeComponent();
30.             forceManipulationsToEnd = false;
31. 
32.             InitOptions();
33. 
34.             // Initialize the transforms that will be used to manipulate the shape
35.             InitManipulationTransforms();
36. 
37.             // Register for the various manipulation events that will occur on the shape
38.             manipulateMe.ManipulationStarted += new ManipulationStartedEventHandler(ManipulateMe_ManipulationStarted);
39.             manipulateMe.ManipulationDelta += new ManipulationDeltaEventHandler(ManipulateMe_ManipulationDelta);
40.             manipulateMe.ManipulationCompleted += new ManipulationCompletedEventHandler(ManipulateMe_ManipulationCompleted);
41.             manipulateMe.ManipulationInertiaStarting += new ManipulationInertiaStartingEventHandler(ManipulateMe_ManipulationInertiaStarting);
42. 
43.             // The ManipulationMode property dictates what manipulation events the element
44.             // will listen to.  This will set it to a limited subset of these events.
45.             manipulateMe.ManipulationMode =
46.                 ManipulationModes.TranslateX |
47.                 ManipulationModes.TranslateY |
48.                 ManipulationModes.Rotate |
49.                 ManipulationModes.TranslateInertia |
50.                 ManipulationModes.RotateInertia;
51.         }
52. 
53.         private void InitManipulationTransforms()
54.         {
55.             transforms = new TransformGroup();
56.             previousTransform = new MatrixTransform() { Matrix = Matrix.Identity };
57.             deltaTransform = new CompositeTransform();
58. 
59.             transforms.Children.Add(previousTransform);
60.             transforms.Children.Add(deltaTransform);
61. 
62.             // Set the render transform on the rect
63.             manipulateMe.RenderTransform = transforms;
64.         }
65. 
66.         // When a manipulation begins, change the color of the object to reflect
67.         // that a manipulation is in progress
68.         void ManipulateMe_ManipulationStarted(object sender, ManipulationStartedRoutedEventArgs e)
69.         {
70.             forceManipulationsToEnd = false;
71.             manipulateMe.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.DeepSkyBlue);
72.         }
73. 
74.         // Process the change resulting from a manipulation
75.         void ManipulateMe_ManipulationDelta(object sender, ManipulationDeltaRoutedEventArgs e)
76.         {
77.             // If the reset button has been pressed, mark the manipulation as completed
78.             if (forceManipulationsToEnd)
79.             {
80.                 e.Complete();
81.                 return;
82.             }
83. 
84.             previousTransform.Matrix = transforms.Value;
85. 
86.             // Get center point for rotation
87.             Point center = previousTransform.TransformPoint(new Point(e.Position.X, e.Position.Y));
88.             deltaTransform.CenterX = center.X;
89.             deltaTransform.CenterY = center.Y;
90. 
91.             // Look at the Delta property of the ManipulationDeltaRoutedEventArgs to retrieve
92.             // the rotation, scale, X, and Y changes
93.             deltaTransform.Rotation = e.Delta.Rotation;
94.             deltaTransform.TranslateX = e.Delta.Translation.X;
95.             deltaTransform.TranslateY = e.Delta.Translation.Y;
96.         }
97. 
98.         // When a manipulation that's a result of inertia begins, change the color of the
99.         // the object to reflect that inertia has taken over
100.         void ManipulateMe_ManipulationInertiaStarting(object sender, ManipulationInertiaStartingRoutedEventArgs e)
101.         {
102.             manipulateMe.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.RoyalBlue);
103.         }
104. 
105.         // When a manipulation has finished, reset the color of the object
106.         void ManipulateMe_ManipulationCompleted(object sender, ManipulationCompletedRoutedEventArgs e)
107.         {
108.             manipulateMe.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.LightGray);
109.         }
110. 
111.         private void movementAxis_Changed(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
112.         {
113.             // Set the object to listen to both X and Y translation events
114.             manipulateMe.ManipulationMode |= ManipulationModes.TranslateX | ManipulationModes.TranslateY;
115. 
116.             ComboBoxItem selectedItem = (ComboBoxItem)((ComboBox)sender).SelectedItem;
117.             switch (selectedItem.Content.ToString())
118.             {
119.                 case "X only":
120.                     // Set the object to not listen for translations on the Y axis
121.                     manipulateMe.ManipulationMode ^= ManipulationModes.TranslateY;
122.                     break;
123.                 case "Y only":
124.                     // Set the object to not listen for translations on the X axis
125.                     manipulateMe.ManipulationMode ^= ManipulationModes.TranslateX;
126.                     break;
127.             }
128.         }
129. 
130.         private void InertiaSwitch_Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
131.         {
132.             // Flip the current TranslateInertia and RotateInertia values in response to the
133.             // InertiaSwitch being toggled
134.             if (manipulateMe != null)
135.             {
136.                 manipulateMe.ManipulationMode ^= ManipulationModes.TranslateInertia | ManipulationModes.RotateInertia;
137.             }
138.         }
139. 
140.         //
141.         // More UI code below
142.         //
143.         private void InitOptions()
144.         {
145.             movementAxis.SelectedIndex = 0;
146.             inertiaSwitch.IsOn = true;
147.         }
148. 
149.         void resetButton_Pressed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
150.         {
151.             forceManipulationsToEnd = true;
152.             manipulateMe.RenderTransform = null;
153.             movementAxis.SelectedIndex = 0;
154.             InitOptions();
155.             InitManipulationTransforms();
156.         }
157.     }
158. }
159.

   This scenario demonstrates how to use the GestureRecognizer class to enable the same interactions on an object as the XAML Manipulations scenario. In this scenario, the GestureSettings property is set to control what types of gestures the GestureRecognizer should react to and then its Process* methods are exercised to interpret user interaction.\n            </TextBlock>\n            <ComboBox Name=\"movementAxis\" Header=\"Movement Axis\" SelectionChanged=\"movementAxis_Changed\" VerticalAlignment=\"Center\" Margin=\"0,12,0,0\">\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"X and Y\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"X only\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Y only\"/>\n            </ComboBox>\n            <ToggleSwitch x:Name=\"InertiaSwitch\" Header=\"Inertia\" Margin=\"0,12,0,0\" Toggled=\"InertiaSwitch_Toggled\"/>\n            <Button x:Name=\"resetButton\" Content=\"Reset\" Margin=\"0,12,0,0\" Click=\"resetButton_Pressed\"/>\n            <Canvas x:Name=\"mainCanvas\" ManipulationMode=\"None\" Margin=\"0,12,0,0\" MinHeight=\"400\">\n                <Border x:Name=\"manipulateMe\" Background=\"LightGray\" Height=\"200\" Width=\"200\" ManipulationMode=\"All\"/>\n            </Canvas>\n        </StackPanel>\n    </ScrollViewer>\n</Page>\n","path":"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run47\\000_BasicInput\\app\\MyWinUIApp\\5-GestureRecognizer.xaml"}
